[289] tool type_text call: {"app":"Telegram","text":"项目介绍区块：常规P0继续以SOL为主：RKC仍是当前质量最稳的新盘之一，成交和流动性承接相对更好，适合作为主监控；WORLDCUP叙事简单、涨幅强，但已经大幅拉升，适合等回踩后二次放量；POPO的流动性和持有人基础相对更健康，维持P0；NOGUY新盘活跃度高但波动更大，降为P0-观察。上线30分钟内固定5个槽位：VANCE是Solana最新profile里的主候选，CA尾缀AAPL但名称是Vance，需要防混淆；CEO China属于名人/宏观梗叙事，早期容易有冲量但持续性未知；MOG是老meme名称复刻，容易吃到名字流量但仿盘风险高；SOD和INDIUM都属于pump系新profile，当前只能作为早期观察，需等成交、LP、交易者分布确认后再升P0。ETH当前30分钟内没有看到比SOL更优的早期P0候选。"}

+];

+

+    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
+  }
+  return response.json();
+}
+
+async function loadState() {
+  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
+    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
+  }
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
+  } catch {
+    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
+  }
+}
+
+async function saveState(state) {
+  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
+}
+
+function chromeExecutable() {
+  const candidates = [
+    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
+    "/App<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1314" />egram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;
+  const response = await fetch(url, {
+    method: "POST",
+    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
+    body: JSON.stringify({
+      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
+      text,
+      disable_web_page_preview: true,
+    }),
+  });
+  if (!response.ok) {
+    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
+  }
+}
+
+function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
+  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
+}
+
+async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
+  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
+  if (fresh.length === 0) return;
+  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
+  await sendTelegram(text);
+  for (const item of fresh) {
+    state.seen[item.ca] = {
+      project: item.project,
+      grade: item.grade,
+      href: item.href,
+      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
+  const now = Date.now();
+  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
+  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
+  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);
+  const text =
+    top.length > 0
+      ? top.map(formatLine).join("\n")
+      : "暂无新的金狗/银狗/铜狗/普通信号。";
+  await sendTelegram(text);
+}
+
+async function tick(state) {
+  const signals = await fetchSignals();
+  await sendNewSignals(signals, state);
+  await sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state);
+  await saveState(state);
+  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const state = await loadState();
+  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
+  while (true) {
+    try {
+      await tick(state);
+    } catch (error) {
+      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
+    }
+    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
+  }
+}
+

+];

+

+  if (!response.ok) {
+    throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}: ${await response.text()}`);
+  }
+  return response.json();
+}
+
+async function loadState() {
+  if (!existsSync(CONFIG.statePath)) {
+    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
+  }
+  try {
+    return JSON.parse(await readFile(CONFIG.statePath, "utf8"));
+  } catch {
+    return { seen: {}, lastSummaryAt: 0 };
+  }
+}
+
+async function saveState(state) {
+  await mkdir(path.dirname(CONFIG.statePath), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(CONFIG.statePath, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2));
+}
+
+function chromeExecutable() {
+  const candidates = [
+    "/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome",
+    "/App<truncated omitted_approx_tokens="1314" />egram.org/bot${CONFIG.telegramToken}/sendMessage`;

+    method: "POST",
+    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
+    body: JSON.stringify({
+      chat_id: CONFIG.telegramChatId,
+      text,
+      disable_web_page_preview: true,
+    }),
+  });
+  if (!response.ok) {
+    throw new Error(`Telegram send failed: ${response.status} ${await response.text()}`);
+  }
+}
+
+function pickNewSignals(signals, state) {
+  return signals.filter((item) => !state.seen[item.ca]);
+}
+
+async function sendNewSignals(signals, state) {
+  const fresh = pickNewSignals(signals, state);
+  if (fresh.length === 0) return;
+  const text = fresh.map(formatLine).join("\n");
+  await sendTelegram(text);
+  for (const item of fresh) {
+    state.seen[item.ca] = {
+      project: item.project,
+      grade: item.grade,
+      href: item.href,
+      firstSeenAt: Date.now(),
+    };
+  }
+}
+
+async function sendSummaryIfDue(signals, state) {
+  const now = Date.now();
+  if (now - Number(state.lastSummaryAt || 0) < CONFIG.summaryMs) return;
+  state.lastSummaryAt = now;
+  const top = signals.slice(0, 20);

+  await sendTelegram(text);
+}
+
+async function tick(state) {

+  console.log(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] scanned=${signals.length}`);
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const state = await loadState();
+  console.log(`DeBot monitor started. poll=${CONFIG.pollMs}ms summary=${CONFIG.summaryMs}ms`);
+  while (true) {
+    try {
+      await tick(state);
+    } catch (error) {
+      console.error(`[${new Date().toISOString()}] ${error.stack || error.message}`);
+    }
+    await sleep(CONFIG.pollMs);
+  }
+}

 const extractionScript = String.raw`
 (() => {
+  const tokenRe = /(?:306326_|pump\.fun\/coin\/)([A-Za-z0-9]+)/;
+  const clean = (value) => (value || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
+  const isBadName = (value, ca) => {
+    const name = clean(value);
+    if (!name) return true;
+    if (/^(solana|pump\.fun|pump\.fun live|ai报告|复制地址)$/i.test(name)) return true;
+    if (name.includes(ca.slice(0, 4))) return true;
+    if (/^[A-Za-z0-9]{6,}$/.test(name) && ca.startsWith(name.slice(0, 6))) return true;
+    return false;
+  };
+  const findName = (link, ca) => {
+    const sameTokenLinks = [...document.querySelectorAll('a')]
+      .filter((candidate) => (candidate.href || '').includes(ca));
+    const direct = sameTokenLinks
+      .flatMap((candidate) => [
+        candidate.innerText,
+        candidate.textContent,
+        candidate.getAttribute('aria-label'),
+        candidate.getAttribute('title'),
+        candidate.getAttribute('description'),
+      ])
+      .map(clean)
+      .find((candidate) => !isBadName(candidate, ca));
+    if (direct) return direct.replace(/^solana\s+/i, '');
+
+    let node = link;
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6 && node; i += 1) {
+      const lines = clean(node.innerText).split(' ');
+      const candidate = lines.find((part) =>
+        !isBadName(part, ca) &&
+        /^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.-]{0,24}$/.test(part) &&
+        !/^(MC|TXs|Top10|ATH|P|Min|Run)$/i.test(part)
+      );
+      if (candidate) return candidate;
+      node = node.parentElement;
+    }
+    return ca.slice(0, 6);
+  };
   const tokenLinks = [
     ...document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="/token/solana/306326_"], a[href*="debot.ai/token/solana/306326_"], a[href*="pump.fun/coin/"]')
   ];

     const href = new URL(link.getAttribute('href'), location.href).href;
